# Initial setup

# main_rite --> The 'main' faith. This is fully defined in 02_mainline_rite_types. 'Rites' are then in 00_rite_types. Christian heresy rites get their own file
# religion --> what used to be the overall religion ie aluk_religion
#{

akom_pagan = { # Akom: the religion of the Akan and Guan of the forest and the coast, and of the Kru peoples to the west
	main_rite = nyame
	faith_details = {
		religion = akom_religion
		color = { 0.9 0.7 0.3 }
		icon = akanism
		reformed_icon = akanism_reformed
		graphical_faith = "pagan_gfx"
	}

	eminent_holy_sites = {
		bono # Bono Manso
		tanoboase # The sacred grove of Tanoboase
	}

	holy_sites = {
		mankessim # Mankessim and the grove of Nananom Mpow
		kumasi # Asantemanso
		begho # Begho
	}

	tenets = {
		tenet_adorcism # Akom, the dance in which the obosom takes hold of the okomfo and speaks through him
		tenet_ritual_celebrations # The yam festival, which honours the dead, gives thanks for the harvest and cleanses the town
		tenet_ancestor_worship # The nsamanfo, the ancestors, who are fed and consulted by their lineage
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_no_head
		doctrine_gender_male_dominated
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic
		doctrine_theocracy_temporal
		doctrine_polygamy
		doctrine_divorce_allowed
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_restricted
		doctrine_clerical_function_alms_and_pacification
		doctrine_clerical_gender_either # The akomfo, who dance and are possessed by the abosom, are men and women
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment
		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_men_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_women_shunned
		doctrine_kinslaying_close_kin_crime
		doctrine_deviancy_shunned
		doctrine_witchcraft_shunned
		doctrine_pilgrimage_local_rites
		doctrine_funeral_stoic
		doctrine_monasticism_absent
		doctrine_sacraments_absent
		doctrine_no_anointment
		doctrine_oral_storytelling
		mz_doctrine_no_syncretism
		unreformed_faith_doctrine
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted
		doctrine_me_ablution_none
	}
}

aluk = { # Sulawesi: Toraja, Bugis-Makassar-Mandar, the central highlands and Minahasa
	main_rite = aluk_to_dolo
	faith_details = {
		religion = hantuism_religion
		color = { 105 72 175 }
		icon = aluk
		reformed_icon = reformed_aluk
		graphical_faith = "pagan_gfx"
	}

	eminent_holy_sites = {
		kandora # Kandora, where the first Toraja puang descended from heaven
		ussu # Ussu in Luwu, where Batara Guru came down from Boting Langiq
	}

	holy_sites = {
		bada # The stone figures of the Bada, Besoa and Napu valleys
		wenang
		siang
	}

	tenets = {
		tenet_ancestor_worship
		tenet_ritual_celebrations
		tenet_megaliths
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_no_head
		doctrine_gender_equal # Bilateral kinship; Bugis and Luwu queens, Wehali reckoned through women
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic
		doctrine_theocracy_temporal
		doctrine_monogamy
		doctrine_divorce_approval
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_cousins
		doctrine_clerical_function_alms_and_pacification
		doctrine_clerical_gender_either
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment
		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_men_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_women_shunned
		doctrine_kinslaying_extended_family_crime
		doctrine_deviancy_shunned
		doctrine_witchcraft_shunned # The po'po', parakang, pongko and suanggi witches were hunted and killed
		doctrine_pilgrimage_local_rites # Offerings at the village's own sacred places; no journeys to distant shrines
		doctrine_funeral_bewailment # Rambu Solo', the Toraja funeral feasts, and the mourning that goes before them
		doctrine_monasticism_absent
		doctrine_sacraments_absent
		doctrine_no_anointment
		doctrine_oral_storytelling
		mz_doctrine_no_syncretism
		unreformed_faith_doctrine
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted # Palm wine (ballo, tuak, sopi)
		doctrine_me_ablution_none
	}
}

	holy_sites = {

		sonba_i
		kelimutu # The three lakes of Kelimutu, where the souls of the Lio go
	}

	tenets = {
		tenet_ancestor_worship
		tenet_megaliths
	}

	tenet_selection_pair = {
		requires_dlc_flag = all_under_heaven
		tenet = tenet_cranial_trophies # The skull trees (andung) of the Sumbanese villages
		fallback_tenet = tenet_human_sacrifice

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_no_head
		doctrine_gender_equal # Bilateral kinship; Bugis and Luwu queens, Wehali reckoned through women

		doctrine_theocracy_temporal
		doctrine_monogamy
		doctrine_divorce_approval
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_cousins
		doctrine_clerical_function_alms_and_pacification
		doctrine_clerical_gender_either
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment
		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_men_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_women_shunned
		doctrine_kinslaying_extended_family_crime
		doctrine_deviancy_shunned
		doctrine_witchcraft_shunned # The po'po', parakang, pongko and suanggi witches were hunted and killed
		doctrine_pilgrimage_local_rites # Offerings at the village's own sacred places; no journeys to distant shrines
		doctrine_funeral_bewailment # The great funerals and the stone tombs dragged by whole villages
		doctrine_monasticism_absent
		doctrine_sacraments_absent
		doctrine_no_anointment
		doctrine_oral_storytelling
		mz_doctrine_no_syncretism
		unreformed_faith_doctrine
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted # Palm wine (ballo, tuak, sopi)
		doctrine_me_ablution_none
	}
}

dayawism = {
	main_rite = dayaw
	faith_details = {
		religion = hantuism_religion
		color = { 125 229 84 }
		icon = dayawism
		reformed_icon = reformed_dayawism
	}

	eminent_holy_sites = {
		kanlaon # Kanlaon on Negros, seat of Laon and of Lalahon
		pinatubo # Pinatubo, seat of Apo Namalyari
	}

	holy_sites = {
		mantalingajan # The sacred mountain of the Palaw'an
		pulag # Pulag, the Cordillera mountain of the dead and of Kabunian
		sandawa # Mount Apo, home of Mandarangan and the Lumad spirits
	}

	tenets = {
		tenet_ancestor_worship
		tenet_mountain_worship
		tenet_adorcism
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_no_head
		doctrine_gender_equal # Daughters inherited equally with sons; women kept their own property and bride-price (Plasencia, Loarca)
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic
		doctrine_theocracy_temporal # The datu rules; the babaylan serves
		doctrine_concubines # One principal wife; datus kept secondary wives and slave women
		doctrine_divorce_allowed # Divorce by return of the bride-price
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_cousins
		doctrine_homosexuality_accepted # The asog, men who lived as women and served as babaylan
		doctrine_adultery_men_shunned # Settled by fines
		doctrine_adultery_women_shunned
		doctrine_kinslaying_close_kin_crime
		doctrine_deviancy_shunned
		doctrine_witchcraft_shunned # Sorcerers (mambabarang, aswang) were feared and killed
		doctrine_clerical_function_alms_and_pacification # The babaylan healed the sick and kept the rites of the dead
		doctrine_clerical_gender_female_only # Babaylan were women, or asog who lived as women
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment # Called by the diwata through illness or dream
		doctrine_pilgrimage_local_rites # Offerings at the village's own sacred places; no journeys to distant shrines
		doctrine_funeral_bewailment # Kin wailed over the dead and kept the mourning taboos (Alcina)
		doctrine_monasticism_absent
		doctrine_sacraments_absent
		doctrine_no_anointment
		doctrine_oral_storytelling
		mz_doctrine_eastern_syncretism # Diwata (Sanskrit devata) and Bathala (bhattara): Indic loanwords for the gods themselves
		unreformed_faith_doctrine
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted # Tuba palm wine, basi and the pangasi rice beer of the feasts
		doctrine_me_ablution_none
	}
}

kaharingan = { # Kaharingan: the Dayak peoples of Borneo
	main_rite = hatalla
	faith_details = {
		religion = hantuism_religion
		color = { 86 239 245 }
		icon = kaharingan
		reformed_icon = reformed_kaharingan
	}

	eminent_holy_sites = {
		bukit_raya # Bukit Raya, where the first Ngaju and Ot Danum came down from the Upper World
		kudat # Kinabalu, Aki Nabalu, where the Kadazan-Dusun dead go
	}

	holy_sites = {
		iban
		taiao
		nunuk_ragang # Nunuk Ragang, the red banyan where the Kadazan-Dusun first lived
	}

	tenets = {
		tenet_ancestor_worship
		tenet_ritual_celebrations
	}

	tenet_selection_pair = {
		requires_dlc_flag = all_under_heaven
		tenet = tenet_cranial_trophies # Heads were needed for the tiwah, the gawai and the baruk
		fallback_tenet = tenet_human_sacrifice
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_no_head
		doctrine_gender_equal # Bilateral kinship; daughters inherit with sons
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic
		doctrine_theocracy_temporal
		doctrine_monogamy
		doctrine_divorce_approval
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_cousins
		doctrine_clerical_function_alms_and_pacification # Healers and singers of the dead, paid by the family that calls them
		doctrine_clerical_gender_either # The basir and the balian, the manang and the dayung
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed # The basir, balian, manang and bobohizan all marry
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment # Called by the spirits in dream or sickness
		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_men_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_women_shunned
		doctrine_kinslaying_extended_family_crime
		doctrine_deviancy_shunned
		doctrine_witchcraft_shunned # The hantuen, witches who take the shape of beasts and eat the living, are hunted down
		doctrine_pilgrimage_local_rites # Offerings at the village's own sacred places; no journeys to distant shrines
		doctrine_funeral_bewailment # The ulit mourning taboos and the tiwah, the feast of the second burial
		doctrine_monasticism_absent
		doctrine_sacraments_absent
		doctrine_no_anointment
		doctrine_oral_storytelling
		mz_doctrine_no_syncretism
		unreformed_faith_doctrine
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted # Tuak and baram rice wine at every feast
		doctrine_me_ablution_none
	}
}

baltic_pagan = { # Baltic: the Lithuanians and Samogitians, the Curonians, the Old Prussians, Sudovians and Galindians, and the Latgalians, Selonians and Semigallians of the Daugava
	main_rite = perkunas
	faith_details = {
		religion = baltic_religion
		color = { 0.6 0.4 0.3 }
		icon = baltic
		reformed_icon = baltic_reformed
	}

	eminent_holy_sites = {
		romow # Romow in Nadruvia, seat of the Kriwe, whom the Prussians honoured like a pope
		sventaragis # The valley of Šventaragis below Vilnius, where the grand dukes were burned on the pyre
	}

	holy_sites = {
		rambynas # Rambynas, the sacred hill above the Nemunas
		birute # The sanctuary on the hill by the sea at Palanga
		staburags # Staburags, the sacred cliff of the Daugava
	}

	tenets = {
		tenet_sacred_childbirth
		tenet_ancestor_worship
		tenet_sanctity_of_nature
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_no_head
		doctrine_gender_male_dominated
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic
		doctrine_theocracy_temporal
		doctrine_concubines
		doctrine_divorce_allowed
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_restricted
		doctrine_clerical_function_alms_and_pacification
		doctrine_clerical_gender_either
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment
		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_men_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_women_shunned
		doctrine_kinslaying_close_kin_crime
		doctrine_deviancy_shunned
		doctrine_witchcraft_shunned
		doctrine_pilgrimage_local_rites
		doctrine_funeral_cremation
		doctrine_monasticism_absent
		doctrine_sacraments_absent
		doctrine_no_anointment
		doctrine_oral_storytelling
		mz_doctrine_christian_syncretism # The grand dukes of Lithuania married their children into the Christian houses of Rus' and Poland
		unreformed_faith_doctrine
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted
		doctrine_me_ablution_full
	}
}

bimoism = { # Bimoism: the Yi (Nuosu) and the other Loloish peoples of Yunnan and southern Sichuan, with the Bai of Erhai
	main_rite = bimo
	faith_details = {
		religion = burmic_religion
		color = { 154 123 93 }
		icon = bimoism
		reformed_icon = reformed_bimoism
	}

	eminent_holy_sites = {
		zizipuwu # Zzyzzypuvu, the homeland from which the Nuosu ancestors set out
		er_hai_lake # Erhai, the lake of Nanzhao and Dali
	}

	holy_sites = {
		jianchang # Jianchang, the Liangshan heart of the Nuosu
		shicheng # Shicheng, seat of the Cuan lords of the Wuman
		ailao_shan # The Ailao mountains above the Honghe, terraced by the Hani
	}

	tenets = {
		tenet_esotericism
		tenet_ancestor_worship
		tenet_adorcism
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_no_head
		doctrine_gender_male_dominated
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic
		doctrine_theocracy_temporal
		doctrine_concubines # Lords kept secondary wives; marriage stayed within one's own caste
		doctrine_divorce_allowed
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_cousins # Cross-cousin marriage was preferred; marriage within the clan was forbidden
		doctrine_clerical_function_recruitment # The bimo cursed the enemy and read the omens before a raid
		doctrine_clerical_gender_male_only # Bimo were men, the office passing from father to son
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment
		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_men_crime
		doctrine_adultery_women_crime
		doctrine_kinslaying_extended_family_crime # The clan avenges its own; feuds ran for generations
		doctrine_deviancy_crime
		doctrine_witchcraft_shunned # Families suspected of sorcery were shunned, and their daughters could not marry out
		doctrine_pilgrimage_local_rites
		doctrine_funeral_cremation # The dead were burned, as the Wuman of Nanzhao had burned theirs
		doctrine_monasticism_absent
		doctrine_sacraments_absent
		doctrine_no_anointment
		doctrine_mythic_literary # The bimo scriptures, written in the Yi script
		mz_doctrine_eastern_syncretism
		unreformed_faith_doctrine
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted # Liquor drunk from lacquered cups at every gathering
		doctrine_me_ablution_none
	}
}

nuo = { # Nuo: the Tujia of the Wuling mountains
	main_rite = nuo
	faith_details = {
		religion = burmic_religion
		color = { 20 33 71 }
		icon = nuo
		reformed_icon = nuo
	}

	eminent_holy_sites = {
		qingjiang # The Yi river (Qingjiang), where Lin Jun, the White Tiger lord of the Ba, rose
		xizhou_pillar # The bronze pillar of Xizhou, raised by Peng Shichou and Ma Xifan in 940
	}

	holy_sites = {
		wuxi
		wuluo_zhongli # Wuluo Zhongli, the mountain where Lin Jun won the lordship of the five Ba clans
		fanjing # Fanjing Shan, the high mountain of the Wuling range
	}

	tenets = {
		tenet_ancestor_worship
		tenet_ritual_celebrations
		tenet_adorcism
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_no_head
		doctrine_gender_male_dominated
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic
		doctrine_theocracy_temporal
		doctrine_concubines
		doctrine_divorce_allowed
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_cousins # A daughter was owed back to her mother's brother's house as a bride
		doctrine_clerical_function_alms_and_pacification # The tima drive out sickness and call down the ancestors
		doctrine_clerical_gender_male_only # The tima are men
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment
		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_men_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_women_crime
		doctrine_kinslaying_close_kin_crime
		doctrine_deviancy_shunned
		doctrine_witchcraft_shunned
		doctrine_pilgrimage_local_rites
		doctrine_funeral_stoic # The dead are sent off with the sayeerh, danced all night around the coffin
		doctrine_monasticism_absent
		doctrine_sacraments_absent
		doctrine_no_anointment
		doctrine_oral_storytelling # The songs of the tima
		mz_doctrine_sinitic_syncretism # The masks, gods and exorcisms of the Chinese Nuo taken into the rites of the tima
		unreformed_faith_doctrine
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted # Rice wine at every rite
		doctrine_me_ablution_none
	}
}

bon_faith = { # Bön: the Yungdrung Bön of the treasure texts brought out by Shenchen Luga in 1017 and of the monasteries that followed Yeru Wensakha (1072)
	main_rite = bon
	faith_details = {
		religion = bon_religion
		color = { 10 235 255 }
		icon = bon
		religious_head = d_yungdrung_bon
	}

	eminent_holy_sites = {
		purang # Tise, the nine-storeyed yungdrung mountain, and the lake Mapham, in Purang
		wensakha # Yeru Wensakha in Tsang, founded in 1072, the chief seat of Bön learning
	}

	holy_sites = {
		kyunglung # Khyunglung Ngulkhar, the Silver Castle of the Garuda Valley, seat of the kings of Zhangzhung
		bonri # Bönri, the Bön mountain of Kongpo
		tengchen # Tengchen in Khyungpo, the Bön heartland of eastern Tibet
	}

	tenets = {
		tenet_esotericism
		tenet_asceticism
		tenet_mountain_worship
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_spiritual_head # The abbots of Yeru Wensakha, of the Dru clan
		doctrine_gender_male_dominated
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic
		doctrine_theocracy_lay_clergy # The Bön clans of Dru, Zhu, Pa and Meu held their monasteries and estates by descent
		doctrine_polygamy
		doctrine_divorce_allowed
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_restricted
		doctrine_clerical_function_alms_and_pacification
		doctrine_clerical_gender_male_only
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed # The married lineage-holders of the Bön clans beside the ordained drangsong
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_fixed_appointment # Abbots held their seats for life
		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_men_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_women_shunned
		doctrine_kinslaying_close_kin_crime
		doctrine_deviancy_shunned
		doctrine_witchcraft_accepted # Rites of subjugation and destruction against enemies and demons
		doctrine_pilgrimage_encouraged # The circuit of Tise, walked counter-clockwise
		doctrine_funeral_sky_burial
		doctrine_monasticism_encouraged # Yeru Wensakha, founded in 1072, and the monasteries that followed it
		doctrine_sacraments_absent
		doctrine_no_anointment

tsuglag = { # Tsuglag: the old religion of Tibet and Zhangzhung, the divine order of the tsenpo, and its survival in the Himalayan valleys
	main_rite = old_bon
	faith_details = {
		religion = bon_religion
		color = { 0.03 0.45 0.42 }
		icon = spirit_bon
		reformed_icon = bon_04
	}

	eminent_holy_sites = {
		yarlha_shampo # Yarlha Shampo, the sku-bla of the Yarlung kings, and Yumbu Lagang below it
		nyenchen_tanglha # Nyenchen Tanglha, the great mountain god of the north
	}

	holy_sites = {
		purang # Tise and the lake Mapham, in Purang
		kyunglung # Khyunglung Ngulkhar, the Silver Castle of the Garuda Valley, seat of the kings of Zhangzhung
		machen # Machen Pomra, the mountain god of Amdo
	}

	tenets = {
		tenet_mystical_birthright
		tenet_mountain_worship
		tenet_sacrificial_ceremonies
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_no_head
		doctrine_gender_male_dominated
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic # Buddhist monks and bönpo priests served side by side at the court of the tsenpo
		doctrine_theocracy_temporal # The tsenpo, come down from heaven by the mu-cord, stood above the priests
		doctrine_polygamy # The tsenpo took queens from the great clans and from Zhangzhung, Nepal and China
		doctrine_divorce_allowed
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_restricted # No marriage within the paternal clan
		doctrine_clerical_function_alms_and_pacification # The bönpo and shen read the omens, healed the sick and performed the rites for the dead
		doctrine_clerical_gender_male_only
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed
		doctrine_clerical_succession_temporal_appointment # The kushen, chaplain of the tsenpo, was named by the king
		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_men_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_women_shunned
		doctrine_kinslaying_close_kin_crime
		doctrine_deviancy_shunned
		doctrine_witchcraft_accepted # Curses, and the counter-rites of the bönpo against them
		doctrine_pilgrimage_local_rites
		doctrine_funeral_bewailment # The funerals of the tsenpo at the tombs of Chongye, with mourning, feasting and sacrifice
		doctrine_monasticism_absent
		doctrine_sacraments_absent
		doctrine_no_anointment
		doctrine_mythic_literary # The funeral and divination texts written in Old Tibetan, found at Dunhuang
		mz_doctrine_eastern_syncretism # Buddhism at the court of the tsenpo from the reign of Songtsen Gampo
		unreformed_faith_doctrine
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted # Chang, the barley beer poured out for the gods
		doctrine_me_ablution_none
	}
}

		doctrine_me_ablution_none
	}

dab_qhuas = { # Kev Dab Qhuas: the Hmong (Miao) of Guizhou, western Hunan and southern Sichuan
	main_rite = dab_qhuas
	faith_details = {
		religion = dab_qhuas_religion
		color = { 115 79 150 }
		icon = dab_qhuas
		reformed_icon = dab_qhuas_reformed
	}

	eminent_holy_sites = {
		dongting # Dongting, the lake country of the Sanmiao, from which the Chinese drove the ancestors of the Miao into the hills
		leigong_shan # Leigong Shan, the Thunder God's mountain above Kaili
	}

	holy_sites = {
		wuxi # The Five Streams of western Hunan, home of the Wuxi Man
		zunyi
		anlong
	}

	tenets = {
		tenet_ancestor_worship
		tenet_adorcism
		tenet_reincarnation
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_no_head
		doctrine_gender_male_dominated # Patrilineal clans; a bride joins her husband's clan and his ancestors
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic
		doctrine_theocracy_temporal
		doctrine_polygamy # A man of standing kept several wives, and took his dead brother's widow as his own
		doctrine_divorce_approval # Settled by the elders of both clans, with the bride-price returned
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_cousins # Marriage within the clan was forbidden; a cousin of another clan was the preferred match
		doctrine_clerical_function_alms_and_pacification # The txiv neeb heal by bringing back the lost soul
		doctrine_clerical_gender_either # Txiv neeb are men and women alike
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment # A txiv neeb is chosen by the spirits through sickness
		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_men_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_women_crime
		doctrine_kinslaying_extended_family_crime # The clan avenges its own
		doctrine_deviancy_shunned
		doctrine_witchcraft_shunned
		doctrine_pilgrimage_local_rites
		doctrine_funeral_bewailment # Days of mourning with the qeej and the drum, while the Qhuab Ke shows the soul the road back to the ancestors
		doctrine_monasticism_absent
		doctrine_sacraments_absent
		doctrine_no_anointment
		doctrine_oral_storytelling # No writing; the history of the clans was kept in songs and funeral chants
		mz_doctrine_sinitic_syncretism # Spirit money and the paper mandates of the dead, taken from the Chinese
		unreformed_faith_doctrine
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted # Rice liquor at every sacrifice
		doctrine_me_ablution_none
	}
}

kiratism = { # Kiratism: the Kirata of the eastern Himalaya, the Yakthung of Limbuwan, the Khambu of Khotang and the Lepcha below Kangchenjunga
	main_rite = mundhum
	faith_details = {
		religion = burmic_religion

	}

	eminent_holy_sites = {
		mukkumlung # Mukkumlung, the holy mountain of the Yakthung
		halesi # Halesi, the cave of Khotang, holy to the Khambu
	}

	holy_sites = {
		kangchenjunga # Kangchenjunga, Sewalungma to the Yakthung and Kongchen to the Lepcha
		ilam
		pashupatinath # Pashupatinath in the Kathmandu valley, where the Kirata kings of the chronicles ruled; Kirateshwar Mahadev stands in its grounds
	}

	tenets = {
		tenet_ancestor_worship
		tenet_mountain_worship
		tenet_ritual_celebrations

		doctrine_theocracy_temporal
		doctrine_concubines
		doctrine_divorce_allowed
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_restricted # No marriage within the clan, nor within three generations on the mother's side
		doctrine_clerical_function_alms_and_pacification # The phedangma and yeba heal the sick, drive out the sogha and lead the dead to the land of the ancestors
		doctrine_clerical_gender_either # The phedangma, samba and yeba are men; the yema are women
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment # A shaman is chosen by the spirits and taught by an older master
		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_men_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_women_shunned
		doctrine_kinslaying_close_kin_crime
		doctrine_deviancy_shunned
		doctrine_witchcraft_shunned
		doctrine_pilgrimage_local_rites
		doctrine_funeral_bewailment # The dead are buried, and the mourning ends with the purification rites that send the soul on its way
		doctrine_monasticism_absent
		doctrine_sacraments_absent
		doctrine_no_anointment
		doctrine_oral_storytelling # The Mundhum, recited from memory by the phedangma and samba
		mz_doctrine_eastern_syncretism # The Kirata of the Indian epics; Birupakshya and Kirateshwar at Pashupati
		unreformed_faith_doctrine
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted # Millet beer offered to the ancestors at every rite
		doctrine_me_ablution_none
	}
}

		doctrine_monasticism_absent
		doctrine_sacraments_absent
		doctrine_no_anointment
		doctrine_oral_storytelling # The chants of the xbi, recited from memory; the Qiang had no script
		mz_doctrine_sinitic_syncretism # The loose-rein prefectures of the Tang and Song among the Qiang of the Min valley
		unreformed_faith_doctrine
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted # Grain wine sucked through bamboo straws from a shared jar at every rite
		doctrine_me_ablution_none
	}

		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment
		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_men_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_women_shunned
		doctrine_kinslaying_close_kin_crime
		doctrine_deviancy_shunned
		doctrine_witchcraft_crime # The kajalio badava finds the witch and breaks her power
		doctrine_pilgrimage_local_rites
		doctrine_funeral_bewailment # The dead are buried, fed on the third and twelfth days, and remembered with carved posts and stones
		doctrine_monasticism_absent
		doctrine_sacraments_absent
		doctrine_no_anointment
		doctrine_oral_storytelling # The Bharath, the Bhil Mahabharata, sung through the night
		mz_doctrine_eastern_syncretism # Kalika and Mahadeo among the gods of the hills
		unreformed_faith_doctrine
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted # Mahua liquor, poured out for the gods and the dead
		doctrine_me_ablution_none
	}

#  BUDDHISM


theravada_faith = {
	# Scripted main Rite: theravada (all start dates). Its core tenets and Rite-specific doctrines are defined on the Rite; faith-intrinsic doctrines remain inline.
	main_rite = theravada
	faith_details = {
		religion = buddhism_religion
		color = { 0.8 0.4 0.0 }
		icon = buddhism
	}

	eminent_holy_sites = {
		bodh_gaya
		atamasthana
		dagon
	}

	holy_sites = {
		bupaya
		sarnath
		kyaikhtiyo # The Kyaiktiyo pagoda on the Golden Rock
		kusinagara # Lumbini and Kusinagara, where the Buddha was born and where he died
		haripunjaya # Haripunjaya, the Mon city of Queen Camadevi and the relic of Phra That Hariphunchai
		sri_pada # Sri Pada, the mountain of the Buddha's footprint
		nakhon_pathom # Nakhon Pathom, the great stupa of Dvaravati
		sukhothai # Sukhothai, the Theravada capital of the Tai kings from 1238
	}
	
	doctrines = {
		rice_theravada_canon_doctrine
		doctrine_polygamy
		doctrine_adultery_men_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_women_shunned
		doctrine_deviancy_shunned
		doctrine_clerical_gender_male_only # The Sinhalese bhikkhuni order did not survive the Chola conquest
		doctrine_clerical_succession_temporal_fixed_appointment # Kings appointed the sangharaja and purified the sangha
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted
		doctrine_me_ablution_none
		mz_doctrine_no_syncretism
	}
	
}

	}

bosnian_church = {
	main_rite = bosnian_church
	faith_details = {
		religion = christianity_religion
		color = { 153 117 134 }
		icon = bosnian_church
		graphical_faith = "catholic_gfx"
	}

	eminent_holy_sites = {
		jerusalem
		visoki
	}

	holy_sites = {
		rome
		ragusa
		esztergom
	}

	#Special Tolerance
	#It should be noted that the Krstjani were never *really* ecumenical. Just isolated rural Christians, mostly forgotten by both the Orthodox and Catholic churches. When they started to become relevant, passive tolerance of them faded into persecution rather quickly.
	#This is reflected in-game by a decision that allows them to be accused of heresy by other ecumenical Christians if they ever become more than just a backwater curiosity. If successful, their "ecumenism" is stripped.
	tenets = {
		tenet_mendicant_preachers # The gosti, its wandering clergy
	}
	tenet_selection_pair = {
		requires_dlc_flag = by_god_alone
		tenet = tenet_anachoresis # Grown from older hermit monasticism: its members lived in small monasteries (hize) and Catholic writers called them monks
		fallback_tenet = tenet_communal_identity
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_monasticism_encouraged # Monasticism was "the highest sacrament"
		special_doctrine_ecumenical_christian
		special_doctrine_is_western_christian_faith
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted
		doctrine_me_ablution_handwashing
		mz_doctrine_no_syncretism
	}

	localization = {
		#Represents the peculiar Bosnian ecclesiastic setup as best as possible.
		HouseOfWorship = bosnian_house_of_worship
		HouseOfWorship2 = bosnian_house_of_worship_2
		HouseOfWorship3 = bosnian_house_of_worship_3
		HouseOfWorshipPlural = bosnian_house_of_worship_plural
		ReligiousHeadName = bosnian_religious_head_title
		ReligiousHeadNameFemale = bosnian_religious_head_title_female
		ReligiousHeadTitleName = bosnian_religious_head_title_name
		DevoteeMale = bosnian_devotee_male
		DevoteeMalePlural = bosnian_devotee_male_plural
		DevoteeFemale = bosnian_devotee_female
		DevoteeFemalePlural = bosnian_devotee_female_plural
		DevoteeNeuter = bosnian_devotee_neuter
		DevoteeNeuterPlural = bosnian_devotee_neuter_plural
		PriestMale = bosnian_priest_male
		PriestMalePlural = bosnian_priest_male_plural
		PriestFemale = bosnian_priest_female
		PriestFemalePlural = bosnian_priest_female_plural
		PriestNeuter = bosnian_priest_neuter
		PriestNeuterPlural = bosnian_priest_neuter_plural

# HINDUISM



vaishnava_faith = {
	# Scripted main Rite: vaishnavism (all start dates). Its core tenets and Rite-specific doctrines are defined on the Rite; faith-intrinsic doctrines remain inline.
	main_rite = vaishnavism
	faith_details = {
		religion = hinduism_religion
		color = { 1.0 0.0 0.0 }
		icon = hinduism
	}

	eminent_holy_sites = {
		varanasi
		ayodhya
		mathura
	}

	holy_sites = {
		kanchipuram
		ujjayini
		khao_khlang_nai
		dwarka # Dvaraka, the city of Krishna
		puri # Puri, the shrine of Jagannatha
		srirangam # Srirangam, the shrine of Ranganatha and seat of Ramanuja
		angkor # Angkor Wat, the temple of Vishnu built by Suryavarman II
		tarumanagara # The Ciaruteun stone, carved with the footprints of Purnavarman as Vishnu
	}

	localization = {
		HighGodName = vaishnavism_high_god_name
		HighGodName2 = vaishnavism_high_god_name_2
		HighGodNamePossessive = vaishnavism_high_god_name_possessive
		HighGodNameSheHe = CHARACTER_SHEHE_HE
		HighGodHerselfHimself = CHARACTER_HIMSELF
		HighGodHerHis = CHARACTER_HERHIS_HIS

		#WealthGod
		WealthGodName = vaishnavism_wealth_god_name
		WealthGodNamePossessive = vaishnavism_wealth_god_name_possessive
		WealthGodSheHe = CHARACTER_SHEHE_SHE
		WealthGodHerHis = CHARACTER_HERHIS_HER
		WealthGodHerHim = CHARACTER_HERHIM_HER

		#KnowledgeGod
		KnowledgeGodName = vaishnavism_knowledge_god_name
		KnowledgeGodNamePossessive = vaishnavism_knowledge_god_name_possessive
		KnowledgeGodSheHe = CHARACTER_SHEHE_HE
		KnowledgeGodHerHis = CHARACTER_HERHIS_HIS
		KnowledgeGodHerHim = CHARACTER_HERHIM_HIM

		#WarGod
		WarGodName = vaishnavism_war_god_name
		WarGodNamePossessive = vaishnavism_war_god_name_possessive
		WarGodSheHe = CHARACTER_SHEHE_HE
		WarGodHerHis = CHARACTER_HERHIS_HIS
		WarGodHerHim = CHARACTER_HERHIM_HIM

		#TricksterGod
		TricksterGodName = vaishnavism_trickster_god_name
		TricksterGodNamePossessive = vaishnavism_trickster_god_name_possessive
		TricksterGodSheHe = CHARACTER_SHEHE_HE
		TricksterGodHerHis = CHARACTER_HERHIS_HIS
		TricksterGodHerHim = CHARACTER_HERHIM_HIM

		GoodGodNames = {
			vaishnavism_high_god_name
			hinduism_high_god_name_alternate
			hinduism_good_god_brahma
			hinduism_good_god_shiva
			hinduism_good_god_krishna
			hinduism_good_god_shakti
			hinduism_good_god_narayana
		}
	}
	doctrines = {
		doctrine_clerical_gender_male_only # The Pancharatra and Vaikhanasa temple priesthoods
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed # Hereditary householder archakas
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_fixed_appointment # Jeeyars and matha swamis held their seats for life
		doctrine_me_intoxication_shunned # The sattvic discipline of the Vaishnavas forbade liquor
		doctrine_me_ablution_full # Ritual bathing, snana, before worship and at the tirthas
		mz_doctrine_no_syncretism
	}
}
